        username: 'testy.automatyczne+rekrutacjaqa@telemedi.com',
        password: 'meUT*2zqg!aHEznUhiC_',
    };
    export const MAIN_PAGE_URL = 'https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl';
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL,
        LOGIN_URL,
        TEST_CREDENTIALS,
    } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export class LoginPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly usernameField: Locator;
        readonly passwordField: Locator;
        readonly loginButton: Locator;
        readonly invalidCredentialsMessage: Locator;
        readonly acceptAllCookiesButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.usernameField = page.locator('#username');
            this.passwordField = page.locator('#password');
            this.loginButton = page.locator('button[type="submit"]');
            this.invalidCredentialsMessage = page.getByText('Nieprawidłowe dane logowania', { exact: false });
            this.acceptAllCookiesButton = page.getByRole('button').filter({
                has: page.getByText('Akceptuję', { exact: true }),
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(LOGIN_URL);
            try {
                await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 1500 });
            } catch {
                return;
            }
            await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithCorrectCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.username);
            await this.passwordField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.password);
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithInvalidCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill('invalid-username');
            await this.passwordField.fill('invalid-password');
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    }
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export class MainPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly bookAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly selectDiseaseDiv: Locator;
        readonly selectMedicationDiv: Locator;
        readonly agreeToAllCheckbox: Locator;
        readonly bookButton: Locator;
        readonly requiredFieldMessage: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child');
            this.selectDiseaseDiv = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.selectMedicationDiv = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.agreeToAllCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.bookButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessage = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
        }
    
        async verifyLoaded() {
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await expect(this.bookAppointmentButton).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-prescription-consultation');
            await this.selectDiseaseDiv.click();
            await this.selectMedicationDiv.click();
        }
    }
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('with invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('with correct credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        await mainPage.bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment();
        await mainPage.agreeToAllCheckbox.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
        await mainPage.bookButton.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//);
      });
    
      // Better to login by API and use the state stored to avoid code duplication
      // and time-consuming login process
      // but for now, it is not covered in onboarding task.
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        await mainPage.bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment();
        await mainPage.bookButton.click();
        await expect(mainPage.requiredFieldMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    });

## Findings

- **High — Test password is hard-coded** in [constants.ts](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/constants/constants.ts:5). Keep it in an Azure secret variable and read it from `process.env`; rotate it if it’s a real credential.
- **High — The booking test likely selects the wrong option** in [mainPage.ts](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/pages/mainPage.ts:21). `button:nth-child(2)` selects “Umów za 0 PLN” in the observed DOM, while the test expects a PayU redirect. That mismatch could explain the `400`. Use a locator for the paid button by its accessible name.
- **Medium — Fixed one-second waits make the booking test flaky** in [m_kot_test.spec.ts](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/tests/m_kot_test.spec.ts:32). Prefer checking the agreement checkbox state after selecting it, then rely on `toHaveURL()`’s retry behavior.
- **Low — Azure only triggers on `master`** in [azure-pipelines.yml](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/azure-pipelines.yml:2). The earlier request specified `main`; include that branch if pushes there should also run the pipeline.

I reviewed the files without running tests.

        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child');
            this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.selectDiseaseDiv = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.selectMedicationDiv = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.agreeToAllCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.bookButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessages = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlotButtons = page.locator('button').filter({ has: page.locator('p', { hasText: 'Umów za' }) });
            this.remoteAppointmentDateSlots = page.locator(`label[for*="${new Date().getFullYear()}"]`);
            this.visitSummaryTime = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item');
            this.visitSummaryDate = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item booked-visit-summary__dates-item--center').first();
            this.visitSummaryName = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__name');
            this.visitSummaryDescription = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__description');
            this.remoteAppointmentBookButton = page.getByText('Opłać za', { exact: false }).first();
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots = page.locator('.select-time__wrapper--time > span:nth-child(2)')
            this.closeReminderPopupButton = page.locator('button[title="Zamknij"]');
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
        }
    
        async closeReminderPopupIfVisible() {
            if (await this.closeReminderPopupButton.isVisible()) {
                await this.closeReminderPopupButton.click();
            }
        }
    
        async bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-prescription-consultation');
            await this.selectDiseaseDiv.click();
            await this.selectMedicationDiv.click();
        }
    
        async bookDefaultRemoteAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentButton.click();
            await this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-consultation');
            if (await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.count() === 0) {
                await this.remoteAppointmentDateSlots.nth(2).click();
            }
            await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().click();
            await expect(this.visitSummaryTime).toBeVisible();
            const bookedTimeSlot = (await this.visitSummaryTime.textContent())?.replace('W przedziale godzin ', '').trim();
            const bookedDate = (await this.visitSummaryDate.textContent())?.replace(/^.*?,\s*/, '').trim();
            const bookedName = (await this.visitSummaryName.textContent())?.trim();
            const bookedDescription = (await this.visitSummaryDescription.textContent())?.trim();
            await this.remoteAppointmentBookButton.click();
    
            return {
            bookedTimeSlot: bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDate: bookedDate,
            bookedName: bookedName,
            bookedDescription: bookedDescription
        };
        }
    
        async verifyRequiredFieldMessages() {
            await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages).toHaveCount(3);
            for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
                await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages.nth(index)).toBeVisible();
            }
        }

    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('with invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('with correct credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        await mainPage.bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment();
        await mainPage.agreeToAllCheckbox.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
        await mainPage.bookButton.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(2000);
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//);
      });
    
      // Better to login by API and use the state stored to avoid code duplication
      // and time-consuming login process
      // but for now, it is not covered in onboarding task.
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        await mainPage.bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment();
        await mainPage.bookButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
      });
    
      test('should create a booking and then cancel it', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        const {
            bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDate,
            bookedName,
            bookedDescription
        } = await mainPage.bookDefaultRemoteAppointment();
      });
    });

**Findings**

- **The test may fail TypeScript checks:** `bookedDay` and `bookedYear` can be `undefined` when their regexes don’t match, but `verifyBookedAppointmentDetails` requires `string` arguments ([mainPage.ts:95–96](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/pages/mainPage.ts:95), [m_kot_test.spec.ts:59–66](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/tests/m_kot_test.spec.ts:59)). Validate the extracted values or make the method handle missing values explicitly.

- **The verification can pass on unrelated content:** it uses broad `span`/`p` selectors with partial `hasText` matches, then checks only the first match ([mainPage.ts:109–115](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/pages/mainPage.ts:109)). Common values like a day or two-digit year could match unrelated page content. Scope these checks to the booked-appointment section and match the intended elements more precisely.

- **The test opens the main page twice:** the test calls `mainPage.open()`, and `verifyBookedAppointmentDetails()` calls it again ([m_kot_test.spec.ts:67](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/tests/m_kot_test.spec.ts:67), [mainPage.ts:109–110](/C:/Users/kotmi/Documents/GitHub/Telemedi-Task/pages/mainPage.ts:109)). Remove one navigation.

    export const LOGIN_URL = 'https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/login';
    export const LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL = 'https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl';
    export const TEST_CREDENTIALS = {
        username: 'testy.automatyczne+rekrutacjaqa@telemedi.com',
        password: 'meUT*2zqg!aHEznUhiC_',
    };
    export const MAIN_PAGE_URL = 'https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl';
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL,
        LOGIN_URL,
        TEST_CREDENTIALS,
    } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export class LoginPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly usernameField: Locator;
        readonly passwordField: Locator;
        readonly loginButton: Locator;
        readonly invalidCredentialsMessage: Locator;
        readonly acceptAllCookiesButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.usernameField = page.locator('#username');
            this.passwordField = page.locator('#password');
            this.loginButton = page.locator('button[type="submit"]');
            this.invalidCredentialsMessage = page.getByText('Nieprawidłowe dane logowania', { exact: false });
            this.acceptAllCookiesButton = page.getByRole('button').filter({
                has: page.getByText('Akceptuję', { exact: true }),
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(LOGIN_URL);
            try {
                await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 1500 });
            } catch {
                return;
            }
            await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithCorrectCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.username);
            await this.passwordField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.password);
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithInvalidCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill('invalid-username');
            await this.passwordField.fill('invalid-password');
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    }
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export class MainPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly bookAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookRemoteAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly selectDiseaseDiv: Locator;
        readonly selectMedicationDiv: Locator;
        readonly agreeToAllCheckbox: Locator;
        readonly bookButton: Locator;
        readonly requiredFieldMessages: Locator;
    
        readonly remoteAppointmentTimeSlotButtons: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentDateSlots: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentConfirmButton: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryTime: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDate: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryName: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDescription: Locator;
    
        readonly closeReminderPopupButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child');
            this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.selectDiseaseDiv = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.selectMedicationDiv = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.agreeToAllCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.bookButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessages = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlotButtons = page.locator('button').filter({ has: page.locator('p', { hasText: 'Umów za' }) });
            this.remoteAppointmentDateSlots = page.locator(`label[for*="${new Date().getFullYear()}"]`);
            this.visitSummaryTime = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item').first();
            this.visitSummaryDate = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item.booked-visit-summary__dates-item--center').first();
            this.visitSummaryName = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__name');
            this.visitSummaryDescription = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__description');
            this.remoteAppointmentConfirmButton = page.getByText('Opłać za', { exact: false }).first();
            this.closeReminderPopupButton = page.locator('button[title="Zamknij"]');
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
        }
    
        async closeReminderPopupIfVisible() {
            if (await this.closeReminderPopupButton.isVisible()) {
                await this.closeReminderPopupButton.click();
            }
        }
    
        async verifyLoaded() {
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
            await expect(this.bookAppointmentButton).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-prescription-consultation');
            await this.selectDiseaseDiv.click();
            await this.selectMedicationDiv.click();
        }
    
        async bookDefaultRemoteAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentButton.click();
            await this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-consultation');
            try {
                await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlotButtons.first().waitFor({
                    state: 'visible',
                    timeout: 5000,
                });
            } catch (timeoutError) {
                console.log('No time slots appeared for today, selecting the next available date.');
                await this.remoteAppointmentDateSlots.nth(2).click();
                await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlotButtons.first().waitFor({
                    state: 'visible',
                    timeout: 10000,
                });
            }
            await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlotButtons.first().click();
            await expect(this.visitSummaryTime).toBeVisible();
            const bookedTimeSlot = (await this.visitSummaryTime.innerText())?.trim().match(/\d{2}:\d{2}(?=-)/)?.[0];
            const bookedDate = (await this.visitSummaryDate.innerText())?.replace(/^.*?,\s*/, '').trim();
            const bookedName = (await this.visitSummaryName.innerText())?.trim();
            const bookedDescription = (await this.visitSummaryDescription.innerText())?.trim();
            const bookedDay = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{1,2}\b/)?.[0];
            const bookedYear = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{4}\b/)?.[0].slice(-2);
    
            if (!bookedTimeSlot || !bookedDay || !bookedYear || !bookedName || !bookedDescription) {
                throw new Error('Booking details are incomplete; unable to verify the created booking.');
            }
    
            await this.remoteAppointmentConfirmButton.click();
    
    
            return {
            bookedTimeSlot: bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDay: bookedDay,
            bookedYear: bookedYear,
            bookedName: bookedName,
            bookedDescription: bookedDescription
            };
        }
    
        async verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedTimeSlot: string, bookedDay: string, bookedYear: string, bookedName: string, bookedDescription: string) {
            await this.open();
            const mainSection = this.page.locator('//main/section');
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: bookedTimeSlot }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: bookedDay }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: bookedYear }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('p').filter({ hasText: bookedName }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('p').filter({ hasText: bookedDescription }).first()).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async verifyRequiredFieldMessages() {
            await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages).toHaveCount(3);
            for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
                await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages.nth(index)).toBeVisible();
            }
        }
    }
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('with invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('with correct credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(LOGIN_SUCCESS_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        await mainPage.bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment();
        await mainPage.agreeToAllCheckbox.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
        await mainPage.bookButton.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(2000);
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//);
      });
    
      // Better to login by API and use the state stored to avoid code duplication
      // and time-consuming login process
      // but for now, it is not covered in onboarding task.
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        await mainPage.bookDefaultPrescriptionAppointment();
        await mainPage.bookButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
      });
    
      test('should create a booking and then verify it', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyLoaded();
        const {
            bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDay,
            bookedYear,
            bookedName,
            bookedDescription
        } = await mainPage.bookDefaultRemoteAppointment();
    
        await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedTimeSlot, bookedDay, bookedYear, bookedName, bookedDescription);
    
      });
    });

      - master

      - script: npx playwright test

    export const LOGIN_PAGE_URL = 'https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/login';
    export const MAIN_PAGE_URL = 'https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl';
    export const TEST_CREDENTIALS = {
        username: 'testy.automatyczne+rekrutacjaqa@telemedi.com',
        password: 'meUT*2zqg!aHEznUhiC_',
    };
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/appConstants';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('should reject invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('should sign in with valid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.allAgreementsCheckbox.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(2000);
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//);
      });
    
      // Better to login by API and use the state stored to avoid code duplication
      // and time-consuming login process
      // but for now, it is not covered in onboarding task.
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
      });
    
      test('should create and verify a remote appointment booking', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        const {
            bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDay,
            bookedYear,
            bookedName,
            bookedDescription
        } = await mainPage.bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment();
    
        await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedTimeSlot, bookedDay, bookedYear, bookedName, bookedDescription);
    
      });
    });

    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/appConstants';
    
    export class MainPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly bookAppointmentMenuButton: Locator;
        readonly bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookRemoteAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly diseaseOption: Locator;
        readonly medicationOption: Locator;
        readonly allAgreementsCheckbox: Locator;
        readonly submitBookingButton: Locator;
        readonly requiredFieldMessages: Locator;
    
        readonly remoteAppointmentTimeSlots: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentDateOptions: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentPaymentButton: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryTime: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDate: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryName: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDescription: Locator;
    
        readonly closeReminderPopupButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentMenuButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child');
            this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.diseaseOption = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.medicationOption = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.allAgreementsCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.submitBookingButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessages = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots = page.locator('button').filter({ has: page.locator('p', { hasText: 'Umów za' }) });
            this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions = page.locator(`label[for*="${new Date().getFullYear()}"]`);
            this.visitSummaryTime = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item').first();
            this.visitSummaryDate = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item.booked-visit-summary__dates-item--center').first();
            this.visitSummaryName = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__name');
            this.visitSummaryDescription = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__description');
            this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton = page.getByText('Opłać za', { exact: false }).first();
            this.closeReminderPopupButton = page.locator('button[title="Zamknij"]');
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
        }
    
        async closeReminderPopupIfVisible() {
            if (await this.closeReminderPopupButton.isVisible()) {
                await this.closeReminderPopupButton.click();
            }
        }
    
        async verifyMainPageLoaded() {
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
            await expect(this.bookAppointmentMenuButton).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-prescription-consultation');
            await this.diseaseOption.click();
            await this.medicationOption.click();
        }
    
        async bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL('https://testyautomatyczne.telemedi.com/pl/make-consultation');
            try {
                await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().waitFor({
                    state: 'visible',
                    timeout: 5000,
                });
            } catch (timeoutError) {
                console.log('No time slots appeared for today, selecting the next available date.');
                await this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions.nth(2).click();
                await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().waitFor({
                    state: 'visible',
                    timeout: 10000,
                });
            }
            await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().click();
            await expect(this.visitSummaryTime).toBeVisible();
            const bookedTimeSlot = (await this.visitSummaryTime.innerText())?.trim().match(/\d{2}:\d{2}(?=-)/)?.[0];
            const bookedDate = (await this.visitSummaryDate.innerText())?.replace(/^.*?,\s*/, '').trim();
            const bookedName = (await this.visitSummaryName.innerText())?.trim();
            const bookedDescription = (await this.visitSummaryDescription.innerText())?.trim();
            const bookedDay = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{1,2}\b/)?.[0];
            const bookedYear = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{4}\b/)?.[0].slice(-2);
    
            if (!bookedTimeSlot || !bookedDay || !bookedYear || !bookedName || !bookedDescription) {
                throw new Error('Booking details are incomplete; unable to verify the created booking.');
            }
    
            await this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton.click();
    
    
            return {
            bookedTimeSlot: bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDay: bookedDay,
            bookedYear: bookedYear,
            bookedName: bookedName,
            bookedDescription: bookedDescription
            };
        }
    
        async verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedTimeSlot: string, bookedDay: string, bookedYear: string, bookedName: string, bookedDescription: string) {
            await this.open();
            const mainSection = this.page.locator('//main/section');
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: bookedTimeSlot }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: bookedDay }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: bookedYear }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('p').filter({ hasText: bookedName }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('p').filter({ hasText: bookedDescription }).first()).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async verifyRequiredFieldMessages() {
            await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages).toHaveCount(3);
            for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
                await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages.nth(index)).toBeVisible();
            }
        }
    }
    
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        LOGIN_PAGE_URL,
        TEST_CREDENTIALS,
    } from '../constants/appConstants';
    
    export class LoginPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly usernameField: Locator;
        readonly passwordField: Locator;
        readonly loginButton: Locator;
        readonly invalidCredentialsMessage: Locator;
        readonly acceptAllCookiesButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.usernameField = page.locator('#username');
            this.passwordField = page.locator('#password');
            this.loginButton = page.locator('button[type="submit"]');
            this.invalidCredentialsMessage = page.getByText('Nieprawidłowe dane logowania', { exact: false });
            this.acceptAllCookiesButton = page.getByRole('button').filter({
                has: page.getByText('Akceptuję', { exact: true }),
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(LOGIN_PAGE_URL);
            try {
                await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 1500 });
            } catch {
                return;
            }
            await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithCorrectCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.username);
            await this.passwordField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.password);
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithInvalidCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill('invalid-username');
            await this.passwordField.fill('invalid-password');
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    }
    
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/appConstants';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('should reject invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('should sign in with valid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.allAgreementsCheckbox.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(1000);
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await page.waitForTimeout(2000);
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//);
      });
    
      // Better to login by API and use the state stored to avoid code duplication
      // and time-consuming login process
      // but for now, it is not covered in onboarding task.
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
      });
    
      test('should create and verify a remote appointment booking', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
    
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        const {
            bookedTimeSlot,
            bookedDay,
            bookedYear,
            bookedName,
            bookedDescription
        } = await mainPage.bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment();
    
        await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedTimeSlot, bookedDay, bookedYear, bookedName, bookedDescription);
    
      });
    });

    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        MAIN_PAGE_URL,
        PRESCRIPTION_CONSULTATION_URL,
        REMOTE_CONSULTATION_URL,
    } from '../constants/urls';
    
    export interface RemoteAppointmentDetails {
        bookedTimeSlot: string;
        bookedDay: string;
        bookedYear: string;
        bookedName: string;
        bookedDescription: string;
    }
    
    export class MainPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly bookAppointmentMenuButton: Locator;
        readonly bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookRemoteAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly diseaseOption: Locator;
        readonly medicationOption: Locator;
        readonly allAgreementsCheckbox: Locator;
        readonly submitBookingButton: Locator;
        readonly requiredFieldMessages: Locator;
    
        readonly remoteAppointmentTimeSlots: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentDateOptions: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentPaymentButton: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryTime: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDate: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryName: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDescription: Locator;
    
        readonly closeReminderPopupButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentMenuButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child');
            this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton = page.locator('[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.diseaseOption = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.medicationOption = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.allAgreementsCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.submitBookingButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessages = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots = page.locator('button').filter({
                has: page.locator('p', { hasText: 'Umów za' }),
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions = page.locator(`label[for*="${new Date().getFullYear()}"]`);
            this.visitSummaryTime = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item').first();
            this.visitSummaryDate = page
                .locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item.booked-visit-summary__dates-item--center')
                .first();
            this.visitSummaryName = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__name');
            this.visitSummaryDescription = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__description');
            this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton = page.getByText('Opłać za', { exact: false }).first();
            this.closeReminderPopupButton = page.locator('button[title="Zamknij"]');
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
        }
    
        async closeReminderPopupIfVisible() {
            if (await this.closeReminderPopupButton.isVisible()) {
                await this.closeReminderPopupButton.click();
            }
        }
    
        async verifyMainPageLoaded() {
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
            await expect(this.bookAppointmentMenuButton).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(PRESCRIPTION_CONSULTATION_URL);
            await this.diseaseOption.click();
            await this.medicationOption.click();
        }
    
        async bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(REMOTE_CONSULTATION_URL);
            try {
                await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().waitFor({
                    state: 'visible',
                    timeout: 5000,
                });
            } catch {
                console.log('No time slots appeared for today, selecting the next available date.');
                await this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions.nth(2).click();
                await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().waitFor({
                    state: 'visible',
                    timeout: 10000,
                });
            }
            await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().click();
            await expect(this.visitSummaryTime).toBeVisible();
            const bookedTimeSlot = (await this.visitSummaryTime.innerText()).trim().match(/\d{2}:\d{2}(?=-)/)?.[0];
            const bookedDate = (await this.visitSummaryDate.innerText()).replace(/^.*?,\s*/, '').trim();
            const bookedName = (await this.visitSummaryName.innerText()).trim();
            const bookedDescription = (await this.visitSummaryDescription.innerText()).trim();
            const bookedDay = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{1,2}\b/)?.[0];
            const bookedYear = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{4}\b/)?.[0].slice(-2);
    
            if (!bookedTimeSlot || !bookedDay || !bookedYear || !bookedName || !bookedDescription) {
                throw new Error('Booking details are incomplete; unable to verify the created booking.');
            }
    
            await this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton.click();
    
            return {
                bookedTimeSlot,
                bookedDay,
                bookedYear,
                bookedName,
                bookedDescription,
            };
        }
    
        async verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(details: RemoteAppointmentDetails) {
            await this.open();
            const mainSection = this.page.locator('//main/section');
            await expect(
                mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: details.bookedTimeSlot }).first(),
            ).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: details.bookedDay }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('span').filter({ hasText: details.bookedYear }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('p').filter({ hasText: details.bookedName }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(mainSection.locator('p').filter({ hasText: details.bookedDescription }).first()).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async verifyRequiredFieldMessages() {
            await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages).toHaveCount(3);
            for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
                await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages.nth(index)).toBeVisible();
            }
        }
    }
    
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/urls';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('should reject invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('should sign in with valid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
      });
    
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.allAgreementsCheckbox.click();
        await expect(mainPage.submitBookingButton).toBeEnabled();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//);
      });
    
      // Better to login by API and use the state stored to avoid code duplication
      // and time-consuming login process
      // but for now, it is not covered in onboarding task.
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
      });
    
      test('should create and verify a remote appointment booking', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        const bookedAppointment = await mainPage.bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment();
        await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedAppointment);
      });
    });
    
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import { LOGIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/urls';
    import { TEST_CREDENTIALS } from '../constants/testCredentials';
    
    export class LoginPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly usernameField: Locator;
        readonly passwordField: Locator;
        readonly loginButton: Locator;
        readonly invalidCredentialsMessage: Locator;
        readonly acceptAllCookiesButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.usernameField = page.locator('#username');
            this.passwordField = page.locator('#password');
            this.loginButton = page.locator('button[type="submit"]');
            this.invalidCredentialsMessage = page.getByText('Nieprawidłowe dane logowania', { exact: false });
            this.acceptAllCookiesButton = page.getByRole('button').filter({
                has: page.getByText('Akceptuję', { exact: true }),
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(LOGIN_PAGE_URL);
            try {
                await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 1500 });
            } catch {
                return;
            }
            await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithCorrectCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.username);
            await this.passwordField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.password);
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithInvalidCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill('invalid-username');
            await this.passwordField.fill('invalid-password');
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    }
    
    import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';
    
    export default defineConfig({
        testDir: './tests',
        fullyParallel: true,
        forbidOnly: !!process.env.CI,
        retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0,
        workers: process.env.CI ? 1 : undefined,
        reporter: 'html',
        use: {
            trace: 'on-first-retry',
        },
        projects: [
            {
                name: 'chromium',
                use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'] },
            },
        ],
    });

    # Telemedi Playwright Tests
    
    This repository contains browser-based end-to-end tests for the Telemedi test site. The tests use Playwright Test with TypeScript and run in Chromium by default.
    
    ## Test coverage
    
    The suite covers:
    
    - Login with valid credentials
    - Rejection of invalid credentials
    - Prescription consultation booking, including validation when required agreements are missing
    - Remote consultation booking and verification of the appointment details
    
    ## Project layout
    
    | Path | Purpose |
    | --- | --- |
    | `tests/` | Playwright test scenarios |
    | `pages/` | Page objects containing page locators and user flows |
    | `constants/constants.ts` | Test site URLs and test account credentials |
    | `playwright.config.ts` | Playwright settings, including the Chromium project |
    | `azure-pipelines.yml` | Azure Pipelines workflow for installing dependencies and running tests |
    
    ## Requirements
    
    - Node.js 20 or later
    - npm
    
    ## Install and run
    
    Install the project dependencies and Chromium browser:
    
    ```sh
    npm ci
    npx playwright install chromium
    ```
    
    Run the full test suite:
    
    ```sh
    npx playwright test
    ```
    
    Run a specific test file:
    
    ```sh
    npx playwright test tests/m_kot_test.spec.ts
    ```
    
    Playwright writes an HTML report after a run. Open the latest report with:
    
    ```sh
    npx playwright show-report
    ```
    
    ## Test account
    
    The login tests read their credentials from `constants/constants.ts`. Use a dedicated test account with access to the test site. Keep real or production credentials out of source control; for shared or production use, load secrets from environment variables or the CI secret store.
    
    ## Azure Pipelines
    
    `azure-pipelines.yml` installs Node.js 20, installs npm dependencies and Playwright browsers, then runs the full suite. Its current trigger is the `master` branch.

    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        MAIN_PAGE_URL,
        PRESCRIPTION_CONSULTATION_URL,
        REMOTE_CONSULTATION_URL,
    } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export interface RemoteAppointmentDetails {
        bookedTimeSlot: string;
        bookedDay: string;
        bookedYear: string;
        bookedName: string;
        bookedDescription: string;
    }
    
    export class MainPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly bookAppointmentMenuButton: Locator;
        readonly bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookRemoteAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly diseaseOption: Locator;
        readonly medicationOption: Locator;
        readonly allAgreementsCheckbox: Locator;
        readonly submitBookingButton: Locator;
        readonly requiredFieldMessages: Locator;
    
        readonly remoteAppointmentTimeSlots: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentDateOptions: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentPaymentButton: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryTime: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDate: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryName: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDescription: Locator;
        readonly visitConfirmedText: Locator;
    
        readonly closeReminderPopupButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentMenuButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator(
                '[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child',
            );
            this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton = page.locator(
                '[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:nth-child(2)',
            );
            this.diseaseOption = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.medicationOption = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.allAgreementsCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.submitBookingButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessages = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots = page.locator('button').filter({
                has: page.locator('p', { hasText: 'Umów za' }),
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions = page.locator(`label[for*="${new Date().getFullYear()}"]`);
            this.visitSummaryTime = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item').nth(1);
            this.visitSummaryDate = page
                .locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item.booked-visit-summary__dates-item--center')
                .first();
            this.visitSummaryName = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__name');
            this.visitSummaryDescription = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__description');
            this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton = page
                .getByText('Opłać za', { exact: false })
                .first();
            this.closeReminderPopupButton = page.locator('button[title="Zamknij"]');
            this.visitConfirmedText = page.locator('p').filter({ hasText: 'Przekierowywanie na stronę operatora płatności' });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
        }
    
        async closeReminderPopupIfVisible() {
            if (await this.closeReminderPopupButton.isVisible()) {
                await this.closeReminderPopupButton.click();
            }
        }
    
        async verifyMainPageLoaded() {
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
            await expect(this.bookAppointmentMenuButton).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(PRESCRIPTION_CONSULTATION_URL);
            await this.diseaseOption.click();
            await this.medicationOption.click();
        }
    
        async bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(REMOTE_CONSULTATION_URL);
            await this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions.nth(1).waitFor({
                state: 'visible',
                timeout: 5000,
            });
            this.page.pause();
            await this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions.nth(1).click();
            await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().click();
            await expect(this.visitSummaryTime).toBeVisible();
            const bookedTimeSlot = (await this.visitSummaryTime.innerText()).trim().match(/\d{2}:\d{2}(?=-)/)?.[0];
            const bookedDate = (await this.visitSummaryDate.innerText()).replace(/^.*?,\s*/, '').trim();
            const bookedName = (await this.visitSummaryName.innerText()).trim();
            const bookedDescription = (await this.visitSummaryDescription.innerText()).trim();
            const bookedDay = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{1,2}\b/)?.[0];
            const bookedYear = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{4}\b/)?.[0].slice(-2);
    
            console.log('Extracted booking details:', {
                bookedTimeSlot,
                bookedDay,
                bookedYear,
                bookedName,
                bookedDescription,
            });
    
            if (!bookedTimeSlot || !bookedDay || !bookedYear || !bookedName || !bookedDescription) {
                this.page.pause();
                throw new Error('Booking details are incomplete; unable to verify the created booking.');
            }
    
            await this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton.click();
            await expect(this.visitConfirmedText).toBeVisible();
    
            return {
                bookedTimeSlot,
                bookedDay,
                bookedYear,
                bookedName,
                bookedDescription,
            };
        }
    
        async verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(details: RemoteAppointmentDetails) {
            await this.open();
            const mainSection = this.page.locator('//main/section');
            const summarySpans = mainSection.locator('span');
            const summaryParagraphs = mainSection.locator('p');
            await expect(summarySpans.filter({ hasText: details.bookedTimeSlot }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summarySpans.filter({ hasText: details.bookedDay }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summarySpans.filter({ hasText: details.bookedYear }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summaryParagraphs.filter({ hasText: details.bookedName }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summaryParagraphs.filter({ hasText: details.bookedDescription }).first()).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async cleanUpBookedAppointments() {
    
        }
    
        async verifyRequiredFieldMessages() {
            await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages).toHaveCount(3);
            for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
                await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages.nth(index)).toBeVisible();
            }
        }
    }
    
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test.describe('Login', () => {
      test('should reject invalid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
        await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      });
    
      test('should sign in with valid credentials', async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
      });
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
      });
    
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.allAgreementsCheckbox.click();
        await expect(mainPage.submitBookingButton).toBeEnabled();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
    
        //payment redirect verification
        /*
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//, {
          timeout: 1000
        });
        */
      });
    
      test('should not book a prescription consultation without required agreements', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
      });
    
      test('should create, verify and delete a remote appointment booking', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        const bookedAppointment = await mainPage.bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment();
        try{
          await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedAppointment);
        }catch (error) {
          console.error('Verification failed due to missing booking popup. Attempting to re-open the main page and verify again.');
          await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedAppointment);
        }
        await mainPage.cleanUpBookedAppointments();
      });
    });

    # Telemedi end-to-end tests
    
    This project automates key user flows on the Telemedi test site. It helps check that login and consultation booking work through the browser, including validation and payment redirection.
    
    ## Tests
    
    - **Invalid login:** confirms that incorrect credentials show an error.
    - **Valid login:** confirms that correct credentials open the main page.
    - **Prescription booking:** fills a prescription consultation and checks that accepting the agreements leads to PayU.
    - **Required agreements:** checks that booking without the required agreements shows validation messages.
    - **Remote appointment:** books an available appointment, then checks its details on the main page.
    
    ## Run tests
    
    ```sh
    npm ci
    npx playwright install chromium
    npx playwright test
    ```
    
    ## Project files
    
    - `tests/` — test scenarios
    - `pages/` — page objects and UI flows
    - `constants/constants.ts` — site URLs and test credentials
    - `playwright.config.ts` — Playwright configuration
    - `azure-pipelines.yml` — CI pipeline
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        MAIN_PAGE_URL,
        PRESCRIPTION_CONSULTATION_URL,
        REMOTE_CONSULTATION_URL,
    } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export interface RemoteAppointmentDetails {
        bookedTimeSlot: string;
        bookedDay: string;
        bookedYear: string;
        bookedName: string;
        bookedDescription: string;
    }
    
    export class MainPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly bookAppointmentMenuButton: Locator;
        readonly bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly bookRemoteAppointmentButton: Locator;
        readonly diseaseOption: Locator;
        readonly medicationOption: Locator;
        readonly allAgreementsCheckbox: Locator;
        readonly submitBookingButton: Locator;
        readonly requiredFieldMessages: Locator;
    
        readonly remoteAppointmentTimeSlots: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentDateOptions: Locator;
        readonly remoteAppointmentPaymentButton: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryTime: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDate: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryName: Locator;
        readonly visitSummaryDescription: Locator;
        readonly visitConfirmedText: Locator;
        readonly cancelAppointmentButtons: Locator;
        readonly confirmCancelAppointmentButton: Locator;
    
        readonly closeReminderPopupButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.bookAppointmentMenuButton = page.getByRole('button', { name: /Umów się/ });
            this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton = page.locator(
                '[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:first-child',
            );
            this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton = page.locator(
                '[aria-label="Menu umawiania konsultacji"] > button:nth-child(2)',
            );
            this.diseaseOption = page.getByText('Alergia');
            this.medicationOption = page.getByText('Aerius');
            this.allAgreementsCheckbox = page.locator('label[for="checkAll"]');
            this.submitBookingButton = page.locator('.buttons-wrapper > button:nth-child(2)');
            this.requiredFieldMessages = page.locator('span').filter({
                hasText: 'Pole jest wymagane',
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots = page.locator('button').filter({
                has: page.locator('p', { hasText: 'Umów za' }),
            });
            this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions = page.locator(`label[for*="${new Date().getFullYear()}"]`);
            this.visitSummaryTime = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item').nth(1);
            this.visitSummaryDate = page
                .locator('.booked-visit-summary__dates-item.booked-visit-summary__dates-item--center')
                .first();
            this.visitSummaryName = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__name');
            this.visitSummaryDescription = page.locator('.booked-visit-summary__description');
            this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton = page
                .getByText('Opłać za', { exact: false })
                .first();
            this.closeReminderPopupButton = page.locator('button[title="Zamknij"]');
            this.visitConfirmedText = page.locator('p').filter({ hasText: 'Przekierowywanie na stronę operatora płatności' });
            this.cancelAppointmentButtons = page.locator('button').filter({
                has: page.locator('span', { hasText: /^Anuluj$/ }),
            });
            this.confirmCancelAppointmentButton = page.locator('button').filter({
                has: page.locator('span', { hasText: /^Tak, odwołuję$/ }),
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
        }
    
        async closeReminderPopupIfVisible() {
            if (await this.closeReminderPopupButton.isVisible()) {
                await this.closeReminderPopupButton.click();
            }
        }
    
        async verifyMainPageLoaded() {
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
            await this.closeReminderPopupIfVisible();
            await expect(this.bookAppointmentMenuButton).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookPrescriptionAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(PRESCRIPTION_CONSULTATION_URL);
            await this.diseaseOption.click();
            await this.medicationOption.click();
        }
    
        async bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment() {
            await this.bookAppointmentMenuButton.click();
            await this.bookRemoteAppointmentButton.click();
            await expect(this.page).toHaveURL(REMOTE_CONSULTATION_URL);
            await this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions.nth(1).waitFor({
                state: 'visible',
                timeout: 5000,
            });
    
            await this.page.waitForTimeout(1000);
            await this.remoteAppointmentDateOptions.nth(1).click();
            await this.remoteAppointmentTimeSlots.first().click();
            //@bug: may fail due to the time options reloading twice (click happens during reload).
            //commented timeout above fixes the flow
            await expect(this.visitSummaryTime).toBeVisible();
            const bookedTimeSlot = (await this.visitSummaryTime.innerText()).trim().match(/\d{2}:\d{2}(?=-)/)?.[0];
            const bookedDate = (await this.visitSummaryDate.innerText()).replace(/^.*?,\s*/, '').trim();
            const bookedName = (await this.visitSummaryName.innerText()).trim();
            const bookedDescription = (await this.visitSummaryDescription.innerText()).trim();
            const bookedDay = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{1,2}\b/)?.[0];
            const bookedYear = bookedDate.match(/\b\d{4}\b/)?.[0].slice(-2);
    
            if (!bookedTimeSlot || !bookedDay || !bookedYear || !bookedName || !bookedDescription) {
                this.page.pause();
                throw new Error('Booking details are incomplete; unable to verify the created booking.');
            }
    
            await this.remoteAppointmentPaymentButton.click();
            await expect(this.visitConfirmedText).toBeVisible();
    
            return {
                bookedTimeSlot,
                bookedDay,
                bookedYear,
                bookedName,
                bookedDescription,
            };
        }
    
        async verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(details: RemoteAppointmentDetails) {
            await this.open();
            const mainSection = this.page.locator('//main/section');
            const summarySpans = mainSection.locator('span');
            const summaryParagraphs = mainSection.locator('p');
            await expect(summarySpans.filter({ hasText: details.bookedTimeSlot }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summarySpans.filter({ hasText: details.bookedDay }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summarySpans.filter({ hasText: details.bookedYear }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summaryParagraphs.filter({ hasText: details.bookedName }).first()).toBeVisible();
            await expect(summaryParagraphs.filter({ hasText: details.bookedDescription }).first()).toBeVisible();
        }
    
        async verifyRequiredFieldMessages() {
            await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages).toHaveCount(3);
            for (let index = 0; index < 3; index++) {
                await expect(this.requiredFieldMessages.nth(index)).toBeVisible();
            }
        }
    }
    
    import { expect, Locator, Page } from '@playwright/test';
    import {
        LOGIN_PAGE_URL,
        TEST_CREDENTIALS,
    } from '../constants/constants';
    
    export class LoginPage {
        readonly page: Page;
        readonly usernameField: Locator;
        readonly passwordField: Locator;
        readonly loginButton: Locator;
        readonly invalidCredentialsMessage: Locator;
        readonly acceptAllCookiesButton: Locator;
    
        constructor(page: Page) {
            this.page = page;
            this.usernameField = page.locator('#username');
            this.passwordField = page.locator('#password');
            this.loginButton = page.locator('button[type="submit"]');
            this.invalidCredentialsMessage = page.getByText('Nieprawidłowe dane logowania', { exact: false });
            this.acceptAllCookiesButton = page.getByRole('button').filter({
                has: page.getByText('Akceptuję', { exact: true }),
            });
        }
    
        async open() {
            await this.page.goto(LOGIN_PAGE_URL);
            try {
                await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 1500 });
            } catch {
                return;
            }
            await this.acceptAllCookiesButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithCorrectCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.username);
            await this.passwordField.fill(TEST_CREDENTIALS.password);
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    
        async loginWithInvalidCredentials() {
            await this.usernameField.fill('invalid-username');
            await this.passwordField.fill('invalid-password');
            await this.loginButton.click();
        }
    }
    
    import { test, expect } from '@playwright/test';
    import { MAIN_PAGE_URL } from '../constants/constants';
    import { LoginPage } from '../pages/loginPage';
    import { MainPage } from '../pages/mainPage';
    
    test('should sign in with valid credentials, reject invalid ones', async ({ page }) => {
      const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
      await loginPage.open();
      await loginPage.loginWithInvalidCredentials();
      await expect(loginPage.invalidCredentialsMessage).toBeVisible();
      await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
      await expect(page).toHaveURL(MAIN_PAGE_URL);
    });
    
    test.describe('Booking', () => {
      test.beforeEach(async ({ page }) => {
        const loginPage = new LoginPage(page);
        await loginPage.open();
        await loginPage.loginWithCorrectCredentials();
      });
    
      test('should book a prescription consultation up to payment redirect', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        await mainPage.startPrescriptionBookingWithDefaults();
        await expect(mainPage.submitBookingButton).toBeEnabled();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
        await mainPage.verifyRequiredFieldMessages();
        await mainPage.allAgreementsCheckbox.click();
        await mainPage.submitBookingButton.click();
    
        //@bug: redirect does not always happen. May fail sometimes
        await expect(page).toHaveURL(/https:\/\/secure\.payu\.com\//, {
          timeout: 5000
        });
      });
    
      test('should create and verify a remote appointment booking', async ({ page }) => {
        const mainPage = new MainPage(page);
        await mainPage.verifyMainPageLoaded();
        const bookedAppointment = await mainPage.bookFirstAvailableRemoteAppointment();
        await mainPage.verifyBookedAppointmentDetails(bookedAppointment);
      });
    });
